<hr data-start="49" data-end="52">
<p data-start="54" data-end="257"><strong data-start="54" data-end="135">1. What is the most common Unicode encoding when moving data between systems?</strong><br data-start="135" data-end="138">
✅ <strong data-start="140" data-end="149">UTF-8</strong><br data-start="149" data-end="152">
<em data-start="152" data-end="257">Explanation: UTF-8 is the most widely used Unicode encoding on the web and for system interoperability.</em></p>
<hr data-start="259" data-end="262">
<p data-start="264" data-end="424"><strong data-start="264" data-end="344">2. What is the ASCII character that is associated with the decimal value 42?</strong><br data-start="344" data-end="347">
✅ <strong data-start="349" data-end="355">*</strong><br data-start="355" data-end="358">
<em data-start="358" data-end="411">Explanation: ASCII 42 corresponds to the asterisk (</em>) character.*</p>
<hr data-start="426" data-end="429">
<p data-start="431" data-end="600"><strong data-start="431" data-end="525">3. What word does the following sequence of numbers represent in ASCII: 108, 105, 115, 116</strong><br data-start="525" data-end="528">
✅ <strong data-start="530" data-end="538">list</strong><br data-start="538" data-end="541">
<em data-start="541" data-end="600">Explanation: 108 = l, 105 = i, 115 = s, 116 = t → "list".</em></p>
<hr data-start="602" data-end="605">
<p data-start="607" data-end="783"><strong data-start="607" data-end="660">4. How are strings stored internally in Python 3?</strong><br data-start="660" data-end="663">
✅ <strong data-start="665" data-end="676">Unicode</strong><br data-start="676" data-end="679">
<em data-start="679" data-end="783">Explanation: In Python 3, strings are stored as Unicode, allowing for international character support.</em></p>
<hr data-start="785" data-end="788">
<p data-start="790" data-end="1063"><strong data-start="790" data-end="943">5. When reading data across the network (i.e. from a URL) in Python 3, what method must be used to convert it to the internal format used by strings?</strong><br data-start="943" data-end="946">
✅ <strong data-start="948" data-end="960">decode()</strong><br data-start="960" data-end="963">
<em data-start="963" data-end="1063">Explanation: Data read from a URL is in bytes and must be converted to a string using <code data-start="1050" data-end="1061">.decode()</code>.</em></p>

<p data-start="60" data-end="208"><strong data-start="60" data-end="111">1. Which came first, the instance or the class?</strong><br data-start="111" data-end="114">
✅ <strong data-start="116" data-end="125">class</strong><br data-start="125" data-end="128">
<em data-start="128" data-end="208">Explanation: The class defines the blueprint for creating instances (objects).</em></p>
<hr data-start="210" data-end="213">
<p data-start="215" data-end="421"><strong data-start="215" data-end="309">2. In Object Oriented Programming, what is another name for the "attributes" of an object?</strong><br data-start="309" data-end="312">
✅ <strong data-start="314" data-end="324">fields</strong><br data-start="324" data-end="327">
<em data-start="327" data-end="421">Explanation: In many OOP languages, attributes are also referred to as fields or properties.</em></p>
<hr data-start="423" data-end="426">
<p data-start="428" data-end="702"><strong data-start="428" data-end="588">3. At the moment of creation of a new object, Python looks at the _________ definition to define the structure and capabilities of the newly created object.</strong><br data-start="588" data-end="591">
✅ <strong data-start="593" data-end="602">class</strong><br data-start="602" data-end="605">
<em data-start="605" data-end="702">Explanation: The class defines how the object should behave and what attributes/methods it has.</em></p>
<hr data-start="704" data-end="707">
<p data-start="709" data-end="970"><strong data-start="709" data-end="783">4. Which of the following is NOT a good synonym for "class" in Python?</strong><br data-start="783" data-end="786">
✅ <strong data-start="788" data-end="801">direction</strong><br data-start="801" data-end="804">
<em data-start="804" data-end="970">Explanation: "Direction" doesn't conceptually relate to what a class represents. "Pattern", "template", and "blueprint" are all commonly used metaphors for a class.</em></p>

What is the syntax to look up the <code data-start="119" data-end="129">fullname</code> attribute in an object stored in the variable <code data-start="176" data-end="185">colleen</code>?</strong><br data-start="188" data-end="191">
✅ <strong data-start="193" data-end="213">colleen.fullname</strong><br data-start="213" data-end="216">
<em data-start="216" data-end="292">Explanation: In Python, object attributes are accessed using dot notation.</em></p>
<hr data-start="294" data-end="297">
<p data-start="299" data-end="495"><strong data-start="299" data-end="406">7. Which of these statements is used to indicate that class A will inherit all the features of class B?</strong><br data-start="406" data-end="409">
✅ <strong data-start="411" data-end="427">class A(B) :</strong><br data-start="427" data-end="430">
<em data-start="430" data-end="495">Explanation: This is the correct Python syntax for inheritance.</em></p>
<hr data-start="497" data-end="500">
<p data-start="502" data-end="667"><strong data-start="502" data-end="582">8. What keyword is used to indicate the start of a method in a Python class?</strong><br data-start="582" data-end="585">
✅ <strong data-start="587" data-end="594">def</strong><br data-start="594" data-end="597">
<em data-start="597" data-end="667">Explanation: <code data-start="611" data-end="616">def</code> is used to define functions or methods in Python.</em></p>
<hr data-start="669" data-end="672">
<p data-start="674" data-end="900"><strong data-start="674" data-end="749">9. What is "self" typically used for in a Python method within a class?</strong><br data-start="749" data-end="752">
✅ <strong data-start="754" data-end="818">To refer to the instance in which the method is being called</strong><br data-start="818" data-end="821">
<em data-start="821" data-end="900">Explanation: <code data-start="835" data-end="841">self</code> refers to the current object (instance) within the class.</em></p>
<hr data-start="902" data-end="905">
<p data-start="907" data-end="1145"><strong data-start="907" data-end="1004">10. What does the Python <code data-start="934" data-end="941">dir()</code> function show when we pass an object into it as a parameter?</strong><br data-start="1004" data-end="1007">
✅ <strong data-start="1009" data-end="1062">It shows the methods and attributes of the object</strong><br data-start="1062" data-end="1065">
<em data-start="1065" data-end="1145">Explanation: <code data-start="1079" data-end="1089">dir(obj)</code> lists all valid attributes and methods for the object.</em></p>
<hr data-start="1147" data-end="1150">
<p data-start="1152" data-end="1374"><strong data-start="1152" data-end="1229">11. Which of the following is rarely used in Object Oriented Programming?</strong><br data-start="1229" data-end="1232">
✅ <strong data-start="1234" data-end="1248">Destructor</strong><br data-start="1248" data-end="1251">
<em data-start="1251" data-end="1374">Explanation: Destructors (<code data-start="1278" data-end="1287">__del__</code>) are seldom needed in Python due to automatic memory management (garbage collection).</em></p>

<p data-start="116" data-end="214">✅ <strong data-start="118" data-end="137">Correct answer:</strong><br data-start="137" data-end="140">
<strong data-start="140" data-end="214">Use the PartyAnimal template to make a new object and assign it to zap</strong></p>
<hr data-start="216" data-end="219">
<p data-start="221" data-end="439"><strong data-start="221" data-end="237">Explanation:</strong><br data-start="237" data-end="240">
When you write <code data-start="255" data-end="276">zap = PartyAnimal()</code>, you're <strong data-start="285" data-end="321">creating a new instance (object)</strong> of the class <code data-start="335" data-end="348">PartyAnimal</code>, and assigning it to the variable <code data-start="383" data-end="388">zap</code>. This is how object instantiation works in Python.</p>

<hr data-start="65" data-end="68">
<p data-start="70" data-end="313"><strong data-start="70" data-end="143">1. Structured Query Language (SQL) is used to (check all that apply):</strong><br data-start="143" data-end="146">
✅ <strong data-start="148" data-end="163">Delete data</strong><br data-start="163" data-end="166">
✅ <strong data-start="168" data-end="183">Insert data</strong><br data-start="183" data-end="186">
✅ <strong data-start="188" data-end="206">Create a table</strong><br data-start="206" data-end="209">
❌ <strong data-start="211" data-end="243">Check Python code for errors</strong><br data-start="243" data-end="246">
<em data-start="246" data-end="313">Explanation: SQL is for working with databases — not Python code.</em></p>
<hr data-start="315" data-end="318">
<p data-start="320" data-end="481"><strong data-start="320" data-end="382">2. Which of these is the right syntax to make a new table?</strong><br data-start="382" data-end="385">
✅ <strong data-start="387" data-end="411">CREATE TABLE people;</strong><br data-start="411" data-end="414">
<em data-start="414" data-end="481">Explanation: This is the correct SQL syntax for creating a table.</em></p>
<hr data-start="483" data-end="486">
<p data-start="488" data-end="639"><strong data-start="488" data-end="554">3. Which SQL command is used to insert a new row into a table?</strong><br data-start="554" data-end="557">
✅ <strong data-start="559" data-end="574">INSERT INTO</strong><br data-start="574" data-end="577">
<em data-start="577" data-end="639">Example: <code data-start="587" data-end="638">INSERT INTO table_name (columns) VALUES (values);</code></em></p>
<hr data-start="641" data-end="644">
<p data-start="646" data-end="807"><strong data-start="646" data-end="712">4. Which command is used to retrieve all records from a table?</strong><br data-start="712" data-end="715">
✅ <strong data-start="717" data-end="740">SELECT * FROM Users</strong><br data-start="740" data-end="743">
<em data-start="743" data-end="807">Explanation: <code data-start="757" data-end="767">SELECT *</code> retrieves all columns for all records.</em></p>
<hr data-start="809" data-end="812">
<p data-start="814" data-end="972"><strong data-start="814" data-end="903">5. Which keyword will cause the results of the query to be displayed in sorted order?</strong><br data-start="903" data-end="906">
✅ <strong data-start="908" data-end="920">ORDER BY</strong><br data-start="920" data-end="923">
<em data-start="923" data-end="972">Example: <code data-start="933" data-end="971">SELECT * FROM table ORDER BY column;</code></em></p>
<hr data-start="974" data-end="977">
<p data-start="979" data-end="1134"><strong data-start="979" data-end="1037">6. In database terminology, another word for table is:</strong><br data-start="1037" data-end="1040">
✅ <strong data-start="1042" data-end="1054">relation</strong><br data-start="1054" data-end="1057">
<em data-start="1057" data-end="1134">Explanation: In relational database theory, a table is called a "relation".</em></p>
<hr data-start="1136" data-end="1139">
<p data-start="1141" data-end="1380"><strong data-start="1141" data-end="1241">7. In a typical online production environment, who has direct access to the production database?</strong><br data-start="1241" data-end="1244">
✅ <strong data-start="1246" data-end="1272">Database Administrator</strong><br data-start="1272" data-end="1275">
<em data-start="1275" data-end="1380">Explanation: DBAs manage and maintain databases. Developers often do not have direct production access.</em></p>
<hr data-start="1382" data-end="1385">
<p data-start="1387" data-end="1570"><strong data-start="1387" data-end="1461">8. Which of the following is the database software used in this class?</strong><br data-start="1461" data-end="1464">
✅ <strong data-start="1466" data-end="1476">SQLite</strong><br data-start="1476" data-end="1479">
<em data-start="1479" data-end="1570">Explanation: SQLite is often used for teaching due to its simplicity and no-server setup.</em></p>
<hr data-start="1572" data-end="1575">
<p data-start="1577" data-end="1786"><strong data-start="1577" data-end="1658">9. What happens if a DELETE command is run on a table without a WHERE clause?</strong><br data-start="1658" data-end="1661">
✅ <strong data-start="1663" data-end="1704">All the rows in the table are deleted</strong><br data-start="1704" data-end="1707">
<em data-start="1707" data-end="1786">Warning: Always use <code data-start="1728" data-end="1735">WHERE</code> unless you're sure you want to delete everything.</em></p>
<hr data-start="1788" data-end="1791">
<p data-start="1793" data-end="2020"><strong data-start="1793" data-end="1893">10. Which of the following commands would update a column named "name" in a table named "Users"?</strong><br data-start="1893" data-end="1896">
✅ <strong data-start="1898" data-end="1944">UPDATE Users SET name='new name' WHERE ...</strong><br data-start="1944" data-end="1947">
<em data-start="1947" data-end="2020">Explanation: This is the correct syntax to update existing data in SQL.</em></p>

What does this SQL command do?  SELECT COUNT(*) FROM Users Hint: This is not from the lecture


It counts the rows in the table Users



It adds a COUNT column to the Users table



It is a syntax errror



It only retrieves the rows of Users if there are at least two rows

<p data-start="126" data-end="222">This SQL command returns a <strong data-start="153" data-end="170">single number</strong>: the <strong data-start="176" data-end="200">total number of rows</strong> in the <code data-start="208" data-end="215">Users</code> table.</p>
<ul data-start="224" data-end="397">
<li data-start="224" data-end="288">
<p data-start="226" data-end="288">It does <strong data-start="234" data-end="241">not</strong> modify the table (so it doesn't add a column).</p>
</li>
<li data-start="289" data-end="320">
<p data-start="291" data-end="320">It is <strong data-start="297" data-end="304">not</strong> a syntax error.</p>
</li>
<li data-start="321" data-end="397">
<p data-start="323" data-end="397">It does <strong data-start="331" data-end="338">not</strong> filter rows — it simply returns the <strong data-start="375" data-end="384">count</strong> of all rows.</p>
</li>

<ul data-start="904" data-end="1021">
<li data-start="904" data-end="1021">
<p data-start="906" data-end="1021">Acest <code data-start="912" data-end="920">SELECT</code> concatenează <code data-start="934" data-end="940">name</code> + <code data-start="943" data-end="948">age</code>, apoi convertește rezultatul în <strong data-start="981" data-end="996">hexadecimal</strong> și sortează rezultatele.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li data-start="1022" data-end="1199">
<p data-start="1025" data-end="1165">Trebuie să iei <strong data-start="1040" data-end="1069">primul rând din rezultate</strong> (cel mai mic în ordine lexicografică după <code data-start="1112" data-end="1115">X</code>) și <strong data-start="1120" data-end="1164">să îl introduci în formularul de pe site</strong>.</p>
<ul data-start="1169" data-end="1199">
<li data-start="1169" data-end="1199">
<p data-start="1171" data-end="1199">Codul va începe cu <code data-start="1190" data-end="1198">416...</code>.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>

</span><span><span class="hljs-comment"># Creează baza de date în memorie sau ca fișier</span></span><span>

</span><span><span class="hljs-comment"># Creează tabelul și inserează datele</span></span><span>
cur.execute(</span><span><span class="hljs-string">'DROP TABLE IF EXISTS Ages'</span></span><span>)

language-python"><span><span><span class="hljs-keyword">import</span></span><span> sqlite3

</span><span><span class="hljs-comment"># Conectare la baza de date</span></span><span>
conn = sqlite3.connect(</span><span><span class="hljs-string">'orgdb.sqlite'</span></span><span>)
cur = conn.cursor()

</span><span><span class="hljs-comment"># Resetare tabel dacă există deja</span></span><span>
cur.execute(</span><span><span class="hljs-string">'DROP TABLE IF EXISTS Counts'</span></span><span>)
cur.execute(</span><span><span class="hljs-string">'CREATE TABLE Counts (org TEXT, count INTEGER)'</span></span><span>)

</span><span><span class="hljs-comment"># Deschide fișierul mbox.txt</span></span><span>
fname = </span><span><span class="hljs-string">'mbox.txt'</span></span><span>
fh = </span><span><span class="hljs-built_in">open</span></span><span>(fname)

</span><span><span class="hljs-keyword">for</span></span><span> line </span><span><span class="hljs-keyword">in</span></span><span> fh:
    </span><span><span class="hljs-keyword">if</span></span><span> </span><span><span class="hljs-keyword">not</span></span><span> line.startswith(</span><span><span class="hljs-string">'From: '</span></span><span>):
        </span><span><span class="hljs-keyword">continue</span></span><span>
    </span><span><span class="hljs-comment"># Extrage domeniul</span></span><span>
    pieces = line.split()
    email = pieces[</span><span><span class="hljs-number">1</span></span><span>]
    domain = email.split(</span><span><span class="hljs-string">'@'</span></span><span>)[</span><span><span class="hljs-number">1</span></span><span>]

    </span><span><span class="hljs-comment"># Inserare sau actualizare în baza de date</span></span><span>
    cur.execute(</span><span><span class="hljs-string">'SELECT count FROM Counts WHERE org = ? '</span></span><span>, (domain,))
    row = cur.fetchone()

    </span><span><span class="hljs-keyword">if</span></span><span> row </span><span><span class="hljs-keyword">is</span></span><span> </span><span><span class="hljs-literal">None</span></span><span>:
        cur.execute(</span><span><span class="hljs-string">'INSERT INTO Counts (org, count) VALUES (?, 1)'</span></span><span>, (domain,))
    </span><span><span class="hljs-keyword">else</span></span><span>:
        cur.execute(</span><span><span class="hljs-string">'UPDATE Counts SET count = count + 1 WHERE org = ?'</span></span><span>, (domain,))

</span><span><span class="hljs-comment"># Commit final</span></span><span>
conn.commit()

</span><span><span class="hljs-comment"># Opțional: afișare top 10 domenii</span></span><span>
sqlstr = </span><span><span class="hljs-string">'SELECT org, count FROM Counts ORDER BY count DESC LIMIT 10'</span></span><span>
</span><span><span class="hljs-keyword">for</span></span><span> row </span><span><span class="hljs-keyword">in</span></span><span> cur.execute(sqlstr):
    </span><span><span class="hljs-built_in">print</span></span><span>(row[</span><span><span class="hljs-number">0</span></span><span>], row[</span><span><span class="hljs-number">1</span></span><span>])

cur.close()
</span></span></code></div></div></pre>

<h2 data-start="2319" data-end="2350">🧪 Ce trebuie să faci exact:</h2>
<ol data-start="2352" data-end="2679">
<li data-start="2352" data-end="2424">
<p data-start="2355" data-end="2424">Descarcă fișierul <code data-start="2373" data-end="2383">mbox.txt</code> și pune-l în același folder cu scriptul.</p>
</li>
<li data-start="2425" data-end="2485">
<p data-start="2428" data-end="2485">Rulează scriptul <code data-start="2445" data-end="2458">orgcount.py</code> (cu <code data-start="2463" data-end="2483">python orgcount.py</code>).</p>
</li>
<li data-start="2486" data-end="2539">
<p data-start="2489" data-end="2539">Se va crea fișierul <code data-start="2509" data-end="2523">orgdb.sqlite</code> în acel folder.</p>
</li>
<li data-start="2540" data-end="2593">
<p data-start="2543" data-end="2593">Încarcă acel fișier <code data-start="2563" data-end="2572">.sqlite</code> pe site-ul cursului.</p>
</li>
<li data-start="2594" data-end="2679">
<p data-start="2597" data-end="2679">Dacă codul e corect, vei primi mesajul: <strong data-start="2637" data-end="2679">"The top organizational count is 536."</strong></p>
</li>
</ol>

2.02441L4.30371 1.97363C4.34801 1.86052 4.45804 1.78333 4.58301 1.7832Z"></path></svg>Edit</button></span></div></div></div><div class="overflow-y-auto p-4" dir="ltr"><code class="whitespace-pre! language-python"><span><span><span class="hljs-keyword">import</span></span><span> sqlite3

</span><span><span class="hljs-comment"># Conectare la baza de date (va fi creat automat dacă nu există)</span></span><span>
conn = sqlite3.connect(</span><span><span class="hljs-string">'orgdb.sqlite'</span></span><span>)
cur = conn.cursor()

</span><span><span class="hljs-comment"># Șterge tabelul dacă există deja (pentru a nu avea date duplicat)</span></span><span>
cur.execute(</span><span><span class="hljs-string">'DROP TABLE IF EXISTS Counts'</span></span><span>)

</span><span><span class="hljs-comment"># Creează tabelul</span></span><span>
cur.execute(</span><span><span class="hljs-string">'CREATE TABLE Counts (org TEXT, count INTEGER)'</span></span><span>)

</span><span><span class="hljs-comment"># Deschide fișierul cu emailuri</span></span><span>
fname = </span><span><span class="hljs-string">'mbox.txt'</span></span><span>
fh = </span><span><span class="hljs-built_in">open</span></span><span>(fname)
</span><span><span class="hljs-keyword">for</span></span><span> line </span><span><span class="hljs-keyword">in</span></span><span> fh:
    </span><span><span class="hljs-keyword">if</span></span><span> </span><span><span class="hljs-keyword">not</span></span><span> line.startswith(</span><span><span class="hljs-string">'From: '</span></span><span>):
        </span><span><span class="hljs-keyword">continue</span></span><span>
    </span><span><span class="hljs-comment"># Extrage domeniul</span></span><span>
    pieces = line.split()
    email = pieces[</span><span><span class="hljs-number">1</span></span><span>]
    domain = email.split(</span><span><span class="hljs-string">'@'</span></span><span>)[</span><span><span class="hljs-number">1</span></span><span>]

    </span><span><span class="hljs-comment"># Caută în DB și actualizează</span></span><span>
    cur.execute(</span><span><span class="hljs-string">'SELECT count FROM Counts WHERE org = ? '</span></span><span>, (domain,))
    row = cur.fetchone()
    </span><span><span class="hljs-keyword">if</span></span><span> row </span><span><span class="hljs-keyword">is</span></span><span> </span><span><span class="hljs-literal">None</span></span><span>:
        cur.execute(</span><span><span class="hljs-string">'INSERT INTO Counts (org, count) VALUES (?, 1)'</span></span><span>, (domain,))
    </span><span><span class="hljs-keyword">else</span></span><span>:
        cur.execute(</span><span><span class="hljs-string">'UPDATE Counts SET count = count + 1 WHERE org = ?'</span></span><span>, (domain,))

</span><span><span class="hljs-comment"># Commit (scrie în fișier)</span></span><span>
conn.commit()

</span><span><span class="hljs-comment"># Afișează topul (opțional)</span></span><span>
sqlstr = </span><span><span class="hljs-string">'SELECT org, count FROM Counts ORDER BY count DESC LIMIT 10'</span></span><span>
</span><span><span class="hljs-keyword">for</span></span><span> row </span><span><span class="hljs-keyword">in</span></span><span> cur.execute(sqlstr):
    </span><span><span class="hljs-built_in">print</span></span><span>(row[</span><span><span class="hljs-number">0</span></span><span>], row[</span><span><span class="hljs-number">1</span></span><span>])

</span><span><span class="hljs-comment"># Închide conexiunea</span></span><span>
cur.close()
</span></span></code></div></div></pre>

<p data-start="960" data-end="986">După ce rulezi, ar trebui:</p>
<ul data-start="987" data-end="1129">
<li data-start="987" data-end="1013">
<p data-start="989" data-end="1013">să nu mai apară eroarea,</p>
</li>
<li data-start="1014" data-end="1074">
<p data-start="1016" data-end="1074">să ți se creeze fișierul <code data-start="1041" data-end="1055">orgdb.sqlite</code> în același folder,</p>
</li>
<li data-start="1075" data-end="1129">
<p data-start="1077" data-end="1129">iar în consolă să apară un top de domenii și numere.</p>
</li>
</ul>

<h3 data-start="169" data-end="192">Te rog să verifici:</h3>
<ol data-start="193" data-end="368">
<li data-start="193" data-end="229">
<p data-start="196" data-end="229">Deschide folderul <code data-start="214" data-end="228">D:\tema noua</code>.</p>
</li>
<li data-start="230" data-end="318">
<p data-start="233" data-end="318">Vezi dacă fișierul se numește <strong data-start="263" data-end="281"><code data-start="265" data-end="279">mbox.txt.txt</code></strong> (Windows uneori ascunde extensiile).</p>
</li>
<li data-start="319" data-end="368">
<p data-start="322" data-end="368">Dacă da, redenumește-l în <strong data-start="348" data-end="356">doar</strong> <code data-start="357" data-end="367">mbox.txt</code>.</p>
</li>
</ol>
<hr data-start="370" data-end="373">
<h2 data-start="375" data-end="419">🔍 VERIFICARE 2: Deschide folderul în CMD</h2>
<p data-start="421" data-end="486">Deschide Command Prompt din folderul exact în care este fișierul.</p>
<h3 data-start="488" data-end="497">Pași:</h3>
<ol data-start="498" data-end="736">
<li data-start="498" data-end="541">
<p data-start="501" data-end="541">Mergi în File Explorer la <code data-start="527" data-end="541">D:\tema noua</code></p>
</li>
<li data-start="542" data-end="643">
<p data-start="545" data-end="643">În bara de sus, scrie <code data-start="567" data-end="572">cmd</code> și apasă Enter – asta va deschide Command Prompt exact în acel folder.</p>
</li>

<h2 data-start="743" data-end="779">✅ Alternativ: trimite-mi fișierul</h2>

<h4 data-start="333" data-end="399">➤ Dacă ai deja <strong data-start="353" data-end="365">mbox.txt</strong> sau <strong data-start="370" data-end="388">mbox-short.txt</strong> în folder:</h4>
<ol data-start="400" data-end="495">
<li data-start="400" data-end="459">
<p data-start="403" data-end="459">Verifică să nu aibă extensia dublă (ex: <code data-start="443" data-end="457">mbox.txt.txt</code>).</p>
</li>
<li data-start="460" data-end="495">
<p data-start="463" data-end="495">Dacă e corect, mergi la pasul 2.</p>
</li>
</ol>
<h4 data-start="497" data-end="527">➤ Dacă <strong data-start="509" data-end="526">nu-l ai deloc</strong>:</h4>
<ol data-start="528" data-end="807">
<li data-start="528" data-end="738">
<p data-start="531" data-end="550">Descarcă-l de aici:</p>
<ul data-start="554" data-end="738">
<li data-start="554" data-end="632">
<p data-start="556" data-end="632"><a data-start="556" data-end="632" class="cursor-pointer" rel="noopener" target="_new">mbox.txt (link direct de pe coursera)</a></p>
</li>
<li data-start="636" data-end="738">
<p data-start="638" data-end="738"><a data-start="638" data-end="738" rel="noopener" target="_new" class="cursor-pointer">mbox-short.txt (variantă mai scurtă, pentru test rapid)</a></p>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li data-start="739" data-end="807">
<p data-start="742" data-end="807">Salvează-l exact în folderul <code data-start="771" data-end="785">D:\tema noua</code>, lângă <code data-start="793" data-end="806">orgcount.py</code>.</p>
</li>
</ol>
<hr data-start="809" data-end="812">
